Retain every\n        // encounter and its dependent rows while widening that constraint.\n        let encounter_schema = self.sql(\"SELECT sql FROM sqlite_master WHERE type = 'table' AND name = 'play_campaign_encounters';\")?;\n        if encounter_schema.contains(\"status = 'active'\") {\n            self.sql(\"PRAGMA legacy_alter_table=ON; DROP TABLE IF EXISTS play_campaign_faction_reputation_totals; DROP TABLE IF EXISTS play_campaign_factions; DROP TABLE IF EXISTS play_campaign_loot_votes; DROP TABLE IF EXISTS play_campaign_loot; DROP TABLE IF EXISTS play_campaign_agenda_npcs;\")?;\n        self.sql(\"DROP TABLE IF EXISTS campaign_session_attendance; DROP TABLE IF EXISTS campaign_session_agenda; DROP TABLE IF EXISTS campaign_sessions; DROP TABLE IF EXISTS crafting_projects;\")?;\n        self.sql(\"DROP TABLE IF EXISTS play_encounter_reward_loot; DROP TABLE IF EXISTS play_encounter_rewards; DROP TABLE IF EXISTS play_encounter_actions; DROP TABLE IF EXISTS play_encounter_conditions; DROP TABLE IF EXISTS play_encounter_ready_actions; DROP TABLE IF EXISTS play_encounter_order; DROP TABLE IF EXISTS campaign_factions; DROP TABLE IF EXISTS quest_milestones; DROP TABLE IF EXISTS campaign_quests; DROP TABLE IF EXISTS campaign_events; DROP TABLE IF EXISTS campaign_characters; DROP TABLE IF EXISTS campaigns; DROP TABLE IF EXISTS monster_tags; DROP TABLE IF EXISTS monsters; DROP TABLE IF EXISTS items; DROP TABLE IF EXISTS game_state; DROP TABLE IF EXISTS schema_meta;\")?;\n        self.create_schema()\n    }\n\n    fn create_campaign(&self, campaign: &Campaign) -> Result<bool, ()> {\n        let exists = self.sql(&format!(\n            \"SELECT 1 FROM campaigns WHERE id = {};\",\n            sql_text(&campaign.id)\n        ))?;\n        if !exists.trim().is_empty() {\n            return Ok(false);\n        }\n        self.sql(&format!(\n            \"INSERT INTO campaigns(id,name,dm) VALUES ({},{},{});\",\n            sql_text(&campaign.id),\n            sql_text(&campaign.name),\n            sql_text(&campaign.dm)\n        Ok(PlayLootResult::Voted { votes_for_recipient })\n    }\n\n    fn assign_play_loot(&self, campaign_id: &str, actor: &str, loot_id: &str) -> Result<PlayLootResult, ()> {\n        match self.play_scene_owner(campaign_id, actor)? {\n            None => return Ok(PlayLootResult::UnknownCampaign),\n            Some(false) => return Ok(PlayLootResult::Forbidden),\n            Some(true) => {}\n        }\n        let row = self.sql(&format!(\"SELECT hex(item_id),quantity,status FROM play_campaign_loot WHERE campaign_id = {} AND loot_id = {};\", sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(loot_id)))?;\n        let mut fields = row.trim().split('|');\n        let Some(item_hex) = fields.next().filter(|value| !value.is_empty()) else { return Ok(PlayLootResult::Missing); };\n        let item_id = decode_sql_text(item_hex)?;\n        let quantity: i64 = fields.next().ok_or(())?.parse().map_err(|_| ())?;\n        let status = fields.next().ok_or(())?;\n        if fields.next().is_some() { return Err(()); }\n        if status != \"open\" { return Ok(PlayLootResult::Conflict); }\n        let tallies = self.sql(&format!(\"SELECT hex(recipient_character_id),COUNT(*) FROM play_campaign_loot_votes WHERE campaign_id = {} AND loot_id = {} GROUP BY recipient_character_id ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC,recipient_character_id ASC;\", sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(loot_id)))?;\n        let mut tallies = tallies.lines();\n        let Some(first) = tallies.next() else { return Ok(PlayLootResult::Conflict); };\n        let (recipient_hex, vote_text) = first.split_once('|').ok_or(())?;\n        let votes: i64 = vote_text.parse().map_err(|_| ())?;\n        if tallies.next().and_then(|line| line.split_once('|').map(|(_, count)| count)) == Some(vote_text) { return Ok(PlayLootResult::Conflict); }\n        let recipient = decode_sql_text(recipient_hex)?;\n        self.sql(&format!(\"BEGIN; UPDATE play_campaign_loot SET status = 'assigned', recipient_character_id = {}, votes = {} WHERE campaign_id = {} AND loot_id = {}; INSERT INTO play_campaign_character_inventory_items(campaign_id,character_id,item_id,quantity) VALUES ({},{},{},{}) ON CONFLICT(campaign_id,character_id,item_id) DO UPDATE SET quantity = quantity + excluded.quantity; COMMIT;\", sql_text(&recipient), votes, sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(loot_id), sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(&recipient), sql_text(&item_id), quantity))?;\n        Ok(PlayLootResult::Assigned(PlayLoot { loot_id: loot_id.to_string(), item_id, quantity, status: \"assigned\".to_string(), recipient_character_id: Some(recipient), votes: Some(votes), vote_tallies: Vec::new() }))\n    }\n\n    fn play_loot(&self, campaign_id: &str, actor: &str, loot_id: &str) -> Result<PlayLootResult, ()> {\n        match self.play_scene_owner(campaign_id, actor)? {\n            None => return Ok(PlayLootResult::UnknownCampaign),\n            Some(true) => {},\n            Some(false) => {\n                let member = self.sql(&format!(\"SELECT 1 FROM play_campaign_members WHERE campaign_id = {} AND username = {};\", sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(actor)))?;\n                if member.trim().is_empty() { return Ok(PlayLootResult::Forbidden); }\n            }\n        }\n        let row = self.sql(&format!(\"SELECT hex(item_id),quantity,status,COALESCE(hex(recipient_character_id),''),COALESCE(votes,-1) FROM play_campaign_loot WHERE campaign_id = {} AND loot_id = {};\", sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(loot_id)))?;\n        let mut fields = row.trim().split('|');\n        let Some(item_hex) = fields.next().filter(|value| !value.is_empty()) else { return Ok(PlayLootResult::Missing); };\n        let item_id = decode_sql_text(item_hex)?;\n        let quantity = fields.next().ok_or(())?.parse().map_err(|_| ())?;\n        let status = fields.next().ok_or(())?.to_string();\n        let recipient_hex = fields.next().ok_or(())?;\n        let votes: i64 = fields.next().ok_or(())?.parse().map_err(|_| ())?;\n        if fields.next().is_some() { return Err(()); }\n        let vote_tallies = self.sql(&format!(\"SELECT hex(recipient_character_id),COUNT(*) FROM play_campaign_loot_votes WHERE campaign_id = {} AND loot_id = {} GROUP BY recipient_character_id ORDER BY recipient_character_id ASC;\", sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(loot_id)))?\n            .lines()\n            .map(|line| {\n                let (recipient_hex, count) = line.split_once('|').ok_or(())?;\n                Ok((decode_sql_text(recipient_hex)?, count.parse().map_err(|_| ())?))\n            })\n            .collect::<Result<Vec<(String, i64)>, ()>>()?;\n        Ok(PlayLootResult::Found(PlayLoot { loot_id: loot_id.to_string(), item_id, quantity, status, recipient_character_id: (!recipient_hex.is_empty()).then(|| decode_sql_text(recipient_hex)).transpose()?, votes: (votes >= 0).then_some(votes), vote_tallies }))\n    }\n\n    fn create_play_agenda_npc(&self, campaign_id: &str, actor: &str, npc: &PlayAgendaNpc) -> Result<PlayAgendaNpcResult, ()> {\n        match self.play_scene_owner(campaign_id, actor)? {\n            None => return Ok(PlayAgendaNpcResult::UnknownCampaign),\n            Some(false) => return Ok(PlayAgendaNpcResult::Forbidden),\n            Some(true) => {}\n        }\n        let exists = self.sql(&format!(\"SELECT 1 FROM play_campaign_agenda_npcs WHERE campaign_id = {} AND npc_id = {};\", sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(&npc.npc_id)))?;\n        if !exists.trim().is_empty() { return Ok(PlayAgendaNpcResult::Conflict);
